# Seed 69 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the real islands trial: no gain beyond the replicates' spread (Iteration 487)

`seed 69 12h widths noevolved islands then experiment footprint.py --fastest --sample 16 --sizes 100,10000 --rounds 3
then experiment review-checks.py spread` asked at 2f1f41c29 (Iteration 464). 44,876 designs, generations 0-175: 32,804
alive, 12,072 screened (27.6% from generation 5), none died. **Islands ran** - 700 island generation lines (seed-facts.py:
"islands: on"), the fix of 464 working.

**The verdict - islands against five plain replicates (seeds 64-68), at generation 109 (the chain's spread):** fastest
0.1274 against 0.1197-0.1298, smallest 14,199 against 14,039-14,207, hypervolume 0.6041 against 0.6027-0.6202, best Q
7.31e+05 against 6.79e+05-7.17e+05 - inside the replicates' spread on every measure but best Q. At its end, generation
175: fastest 0.1125, smallest 14,043 - the fastest end of the six runs (seed 65's 0.115 next), within the ~5% the
replicates spread. **The prediction (464) fails on speed**: its fastest at 109 was not below 0.1197, so by its own rule
islands are not worth keeping; seed 70 runs without them.

Facts: the front 147 designs (115 by mutation, 16 borrowed, 16 by crossover); the JIT on 86 of them; at 32 bits 34,240
designs measured, none failed. Held out: sieve's Spearman with the selection 0.968 (fastest third 0.932, tenth 0.710),
regret +2.9%. The fast end's optimism: the fastest tenth re-measured / in-run 1.096. Remote checks: dream 0.963
(default build, 24 designs), 0.862 (32-bit, 30), 0.863 (64-bit, 30); the Tegra 0.965 (16) - against the laptop's 32-bit
speeds, dream 0.928 and the Tegra 0.881. Across the three machine classes the best of four designs measured on all:
bccdee5462 (0.449 of s6, geometric mean). The footprint experiment: the 16 fastest's order does not hold for program
sizes - Spearman -0.171 at 100 words, -0.329 at 10,000. Gene votes: jitos a candidate (12 of 12 over seeds 67-69).

---

# Evolved VM designs

32804 designs evaluated, 32804 alive, 12072 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 67ef18152b, speed 0.137, binary + image 29,496, memory 172 KB - Q 0.210 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
